# DECISIONS.md — the log

Format: `#N · date · decision · rationale/reference`
Locked means locked. Reopen only with user data or Timothy's explicit call.

## Locked

1. 2026-08-08 · Product = daily speech gym (skill trait), not deadline prep · founder is user zero; persona-wedge over context-wedge
2. 2026-08-08 · Persona: ambitious 16–28 self-improvement demographic · founder-identical; reachable via content channels
3. 2026-08-08 · Freemium, monthly + annual (annual pushed) · Duolingo/Elevate pattern; capture the year while motivation is high
4. 2026-08-08 · Palette: terracotta #E76F51 CTA, amber earned-only, stone/cream room · brand.md; one orange per screen
5. 2026-08-08 · Type: Space Grotesk display/numbers, Inter body, Space Mono data labels · numbers are the hero; mono kills wellness-softness
6. 2026-08-09 · Name: **Ethos** · Aristotle's speaker-credibility; passes say/spell filter (filter itself is locked: non-founder says + spells first try)
7. 2026-08-09 · Mascot: red panda "Demos", flat, no outlines, cute not smug · approved art = assets/demos-side-profile.png; Higgsfield ref element `demos-red-panda`
8. 2026-08-09 · Signature element: the pause bar — silence rendered amber as achievement · core product insight made visible; no competitor has it
9. 2026-08-09 · Home layout: "The Floor" — one dominant rep card · opening the app = being handed the floor
10. 2026-08-09 · Stars = objective metric thresholds only, never participation · measure-don't-flatter
11. 2026-08-09 · Paywall placement: after day-3 progress card; minimal onboarding, never a quiz-wall · Wellspoken's quiz-wall is a documented resentment point
12. 2026-08-09 · Supply layer: one word/phrase upgrade per rep from user's own transcript → personal lexicon · Wellspoken users' most-praised feature; measurement + supply = coaching
13. 2026-08-09 · Boss modes (Cold Topic, Debate, Hostile Q&A) are premium weekly content, not the daily loop · protects the 5-minute habit
14. 2026-08-09 · No pay-to-win; freezes capped at 2 equipped · currency buys convenience and challenge, never truth
15. 2026-08-09 · Accounts: Supabase auth, anonymous-first — rep 1 before signup, gate at "save progress" · quiz-wall lesson applied to auth
16. 2026-08-09 · XP = effort currency (reps, boss/mod multipliers) feeding weekly ~20-person leagues; stars remain the ONLY quality signal; XP unbuyable · leaderboard rewards grinding without corrupting scores
17. 2026-08-09 · History = training-log layout (design direction B); free 7 days, premium full archive + comparison cards
18. 2026-08-09 · Ethos Index /1000: 8 dimensions, two tiers — measured (Pause 150, Fillers 150, Pace 100, Range 100) + judged (Structure 150, Credibility 150, Engagement 100, Confidence 100) · the score IS the brand ("your Ethos"); big denominator makes progress visible
19. 2026-08-09 · Judged scores must cite ≥1 quoted moment/timestamp or the LLM output is rejected and re-run; Credibility and Confidence get deterministic anchors (hedge/restart counts) · no-horoscope rule enforced in schema, not just prose
20. 2026-08-09 · "Entertaining" folded into Engagement · same underlying behaviors; separate scores would double-count
21. 2026-08-09 · WPM zone: 130–160 = "in the zone", below = "strolling", above = "sprinting" · aligned to the Index pace dimension (mechanics.md); one zone everywhere
22. 2026-08-09 · Pause bar rendering: composed (pre-sentence) held pause = amber pill; mid-sentence held pause = stone pill; beats = dots; speech = ~2s ticks · amber stays earned-only — mid-sentence silence is shown, not celebrated
23. 2026-08-09 · Coach layer is best-effort: LLM output that fails voice/grounding validation retries twice then falls back to a deterministic coach line built from metrics · the numbers are the product; the LLM never blocks or invents them
24. 2026-08-09 · Engine LLM: claude-opus-5, strict JSON schema, low effort, server-side refusal fallback · one call per rep; latency inside a ≤5-min loop

25. 2026-08-09 · Bottom nav = Today / Path / Log / You, label-only, hidden on the floor and marketing · icons would compete with the one terracotta tap; the rep screen stays undistracted
26. 2026-08-09 · Streak: a day counts once; today keeps yesterday's streak alive but 'at risk' until the rep lands · loss-aversion framing allowed, guilt is not — no sad mascot, no shame state
27. 2026-08-09 · XP curve: each level costs 50 more than the last (L2 50, L3 150, L4 300) · first level-up inside week one at 10 XP/rep, then a real grind; calibrate in beta
28. 2026-08-09 · Unit unlocks gate on cumulative stars (Pace 4, Pause 8, Cold Topic 12) · progression earned by measured quality, never by attendance
29. 2026-08-09 · Achievements name the number that unlocked them · a badge without a number is a participation trophy
30. 2026-08-09 · Insights are pure arithmetic over stored reps, never an LLM call · cross-rep claims must be as checkable as single-rep ones
31. 2026-08-09 · Free tier: 7 days of log, 3 lexicon entries; premium unlocks archive, boss modes, full pause analytics · mechanics.md monetisation, paywall still only after the day-3 card. The 3-entry lexicon cap is superseded by #507: Say it better is free and uncapped.
32. 2026-08-09 · Audio replay marks fillers and held pauses on the timeline, tap to seek · the user hears the evidence instead of taking our word for it
33. 2026-08-09 · Service worker caches shell + assets; /api/analyze and Supabase never cached · a rep that can't reach the engine fails honestly rather than showing stale numbers
34. 2026-08-09 · One celebration moment per rep (streak count, amber, ~2s, dismissible) · Demos celebrates there and nowhere else in the loop

35. 2026-08-10 · "Frame" step ships as an opt-in setting, 30s, OFF by default · moved up from the open queue; trains think-before-speak without taxing the ≤5-min loop for people who don't want it
36. 2026-08-10 · Boss: free gets this week's Cold Topic once; premium unlocks the library (any topic, any time) · keeps DECISIONS #13's intent — premium buys depth and repeats, not the first taste; a locked button nobody can press is not a monetisation strategy
37. 2026-08-10 · Stress mods multiply XP, never stars · corrects the original stress-mods note, which contradicted #10 and #16 — difficulty buys effort credit, quality stays measured
38. 2026-08-10 · Freezes are EARNED (one per full week of streak, 2 max), auto-spend on the gap, and bridge a day without counting toward the streak · money never buys streaks; a frozen day protected you, it didn't train you
39. 2026-08-10 · A freeze is only spent when it can close the WHOLE gap · half-bridging leaves the streak broken anyway, so spending would be theft
40. 2026-08-10 · Boss accuracy = coverage of ground truth − penalty for wrong claims (18 confident, 6 hedged) · arithmetic over cited verdicts, so the number is re-derivable from the stored report; hedging a wrong claim costs less than asserting it
41. 2026-08-10 · The server recomputes mods and the XP multiplier from the account's entitlement; the client's claim is advisory · a hand-edited URL can change how hard your rep is, never what it pays
42. 2026-08-10 · Settings names the reminder's real scheduling tier (OS-scheduled / tab-only / unsupported) · a reminder that silently never fires is worse than no reminder

43. 2026-08-10 · The path lives on the first screen, directly under the rep card · Amabile & Kramer (12,000 diary entries): nothing moves motivation like visible progress in meaningful work. The Floor keeps its one dominant action; the rail answers "where am I" underneath it
44. 2026-08-10 · Milestones are ordered by PROXIMITY, nearest first, always with an exact "N to go" · goal-gradient — perceived closeness is what raises effort, so the ordering is the feature, not a detail (asserted in tests)
45. 2026-08-10 · Endowed progress via one honest pre-filled node ("Showed up"), worth zero stars · Nunes & Drèze 2006 got 34% vs 19% completion with a head start customers could see was free — the reframe from "not begun" to "underway" does the work, not concealment. No free stars, ever
46. 2026-08-10 · Every reward names the number it measures — a milestone with no number does not ship · SDT gamification meta-analysis (35 studies, ~2,500 people): rewards lift motivation when INFORMATIONAL, undermine it when controlling, and did not improve competence satisfaction at all. Hattie & Timperley rank self-level praise among the least effective feedback. Reward often; never reward emptily
47. 2026-08-10 · Perceived progress must never exceed real progress · rejected the "hook them even if it isn't real" framing after the research pass: fluency illusion raises felt learning without raising learning, and for a speech gym the illusion is discovered in public. Also protects the comparison card, which works because both recordings are real (docs/progression-research.md)
48. 2026-08-10 · Anticipation gets its own surface: what the rep will earn is shown BEFORE the record button · dopamine fires on cues that predict reward, not only on reward, and the two act independently — the pre-rep cue is a motivator in its own right, not a preview
49. 2026-08-10 · Near-misses (milestone ≥85%) are surfaced, not hidden · near-miss outcomes recruit win circuitry and raise the pull to go again; the honest version is simply showing how close the number already is
50. 2026-08-10 · One open loop is named on the home screen — the started-but-unfinished lesson · Zeigarnik 1927: incomplete tasks hold psychological tension and stay mentally active
51. 2026-08-10 · Mondays, the 1st and Jan 1 get fresh-start framing, pointing forward only · Dai, Milkman & Riis 2014: temporal landmarks open a new mental accounting period (+33% exercise at week start). The mechanism is the CLOSED chapter, so the copy never mentions what was missed (asserted in tests)
52. 2026-08-10 · Every results screen ends on something true and good — the streak-end rule · PNAS, 14,383 volunteers over 1.97M tasks: people weight the END of a sequence disproportionately when deciding whether to return, and hard endings drive quitting. `endNote()` always returns a moment and never ends on a criticism
53. 2026-08-10 · Personal bests compare against a snapshot captured BEFORE the rep, never a post-rep list minus its last row · the slice looks equivalent and isn't: when a rep fails to persist it silently discards a real prior rep and understates the record
54. 2026-08-10 · Stars are capped by SUBSTANCE (word count, distinct-word ratio, repeated-phrase share) before the filler rate is consulted · reported by Timothy: "I don't know" eight times has zero fillers, a clean pace and used to score three stars. Filler rate alone measures how cleanly you said nothing. Substance can only ever LOWER stars — variety never buys one the filler rate didn't earn
55. 2026-08-10 · Below the substance floor there is NO Ethos Index and no coach call — "Not enough to score", not a low score · a partial number over an empty rep makes every other number on the screen a lie; and the LLM has nothing to judge
56. 2026-08-10 · The transcript is shown open on the results screen, never behind a disclosure · every score is a claim about those words, so they have to be readable without going looking for them

57. 2026-08-10 · ~~Home is a four-task daily set~~ REVERSED same day (Timothy: "too much of a copy") — home stays The Floor, one dominant rep card per DECISIONS #9. A checklist home is Wellspoken's structural signature, and adopting it demoted our own rep card to one row in a menu. Original entry: home is a four-task daily set, one of which records · Wellspoken's checklist home is the thing that makes it feel designed (researched 10 Aug). Ethos copies the SHAPE, not the contents: vision.md still caps the loop at 5 minutes and DECISIONS #9 keeps the rep dominant, so the other three tasks are under a minute and none of them record
58. 2026-08-10 · ~~REVERSED with #57.~~ Every task in the set must be completable for real, or it shows why it's locked · a checklist row that can't be ticked is worse than three that can; an unavailable task is never counted as done
59. 2026-08-10 · Topic roulette REPLACES the floor card rather than sitting beside it · a second card means a second terracotta button, and brand.md allows exactly one tap per screen. The mechanic earns its place because choosing your own topic lets you quietly rehearse while deciding — the one thing a cold-open drill can't allow
60. 2026-08-10 · Structure tips are per SHAPE (opinion / story / explain / pitch), never per topic · we honestly know what shape of answer the prompt asks for and nothing more; per-topic advice would be invention
61. 2026-08-10 · ~~REVERSED with #57~~ — insights already live on /history, so a separate check-in card was duplicate surface. The reasoning stands if it ever returns: one arithmetic line from stored reps, not a chat · Wellspoken's is an AI coach conversation; a conversation invites generated encouragement, which is the horoscope feedback vision.md bans (DECISIONS #30 already forbids LLM cross-rep claims)
63. 2026-08-10 · Lexicon flash lives on /you beside the lexicon it tests, not on home · retrieval practice on our own supply layer is ours; putting it in a home checklist was what made it read as a copy. Superseded by #507: the test lives on Say it better's own page, opened from You's first card.
64. 2026-08-10 · Dark mode ships behind an explicit toggle (System / Light / Dark), light stays the default · brand.md deferred dark until the loop retains; Timothy's call overrides that, but making it opt-in keeps the designed surface the one most people see. Warm neutrals only in both — no cool greys, no pure black
65. 2026-08-10 · Terracotta and amber are IDENTICAL in both themes · they carry meaning ("tap this", "you earned this"); a colour that means something has to read the same everywhere. Only the neutrals swap
66. 2026-08-10 · Tomorrow's focus is chosen by per-skill decay, and the reason always names the number · Duolingo's published answer to "why come back" is half-life regression (Settles & Meeder, ACL 2016, 45%+ error reduction): the app holds a model of what YOU are about to lose and schedules against it. Ours runs over the four MEASURED Index dimensions, so it stays arithmetic (DECISIONS #30). Half-life of 7 days is a guess — Duolingo FITS theirs from millions of traces; calibrate on real reps
62. 2026-08-10 · NO pronunciation score · Wellspoken scores it, but Whisper gives us words and timings, not phonemes — we cannot measure it, so scoring it would be the exact fabrication the no-horoscope rule exists to prevent
67. 2026-08-10 · Demos ships with a real alpha channel, cut from the 1024² masters and downscaled to 512² · shipping him on an opaque background forced a theme workaround per placement — a cream tile in dark, and no Demos at all on the floor card, which is the one screen he's meant to be on. Transparency is the fix at the asset, not in CSS; `scripts/cut-demos-alpha.mjs` re-runs it, always from `assets/`, never from the already-cut `public/` copies

68. 2026-08-11 · Delivery feedback is IN and mainline: every rep offers Voice / Voice + Video, equal weight, no premium badge on the toggle, sticky per drill type · daily defaults OFF (the loop's value is that it works on a tram with headphones), boss defaults ON (being watched is the point). Rep 1 is audio ALWAYS — camera permission before the product has visibly worked is the most expensive ask in the funnel
69. 2026-08-11 · Video produces a SECOND score, "Presence" /1000, beside the Index at the same size with its own history — it never touches the Ethos Index · the Index stays audio-only so the trendline and the XP leagues stay comparable whichever mode someone picked that day. Two scores, not one score with an asterisk (asserted in tests)
70. 2026-08-11 · Pose detection runs client-side (MediaPipe Pose, WASM, ~30fps); raw video is never uploaded and never stored; only five derived numbers persist · the uploaded blob is built from `stream.getAudioTracks()`, never from the camera stream — that one line is what makes the claim true rather than aspirational. Stated plainly in the UI as a selling point, not fine print
71. 2026-08-11 · The pose runtime and model are SELF-HOSTED, staged into `public/pose/` at build time and gitignored · loading them from Google's CDN would announce every video rep to a third party before a frame was analysed, which makes "video never leaves your device" technically true and practically weaselly. Build-time failure is non-fatal: the toggle reports Voice + Video unavailable rather than recording video it can't measure
72. 2026-08-11 · Presence is trusted from the client and recomputed nowhere · the server has no frames by construction, and unlike the mods (#41) that's safe precisely because Presence feeds nothing — not the Index, not stars, not XP, not the league. A forged Presence lies only to the forger
73. 2026-08-11 · Premium splits on the COST line, not the feature line: the live in-rep ring is free forever; Pro buys the Presence score, timestamped moments, the week-over-week trendline and playback with markers · pose is local compute at zero marginal cost, and a free user *seeing* the camera work and wanting the readout is a stronger upgrade prompt than a padlock — it also gives Pro a reason to exist beyond "more reps per week", which is the paywall every competitor already uses
74. 2026-08-11 · Playback with markers is a LOCAL object URL that dies with the screen; the log shows Presence numbers and timestamps but never video · there is no video library because there is no video anywhere. Said out loud on the playback card rather than left to be assumed
75. 2026-08-11 · Meter the JUDGED tier, never the rep: measured metrics + transcript + the live ring are free and unlimited; the LLM layer is 1/day free, rollover capped at 3, premium unlimited · reps are free to serve, judging isn't. Every rep returns *something* — a rep that returns nothing is a chore, and the free tier still has to build a habit. Superseded in part by #514: a free account gets 3 scored recordings a day, fresh each day, with no roll-over.
76. 2026-08-11 · Two metering guardrails, both asserted in tests: streaks count reps not analyses, and an analysis is never charged for unless it was delivered · the cap must never break a streak (punishing the behaviour you want is rage-inducing), and a capped rep is still stored, still counted, still measured. A near-silent rep never spends one either — there'd be nothing to judge
77. 2026-08-11 · The judged allowance resets on the user's LOCAL calendar day, from a client-sent UTC offset clamped to ±14h · UTC would reset mid-morning in Sydney. Forging the offset shifts the boundary by hours, never the rate: backwards forges grant nothing (elapsed days floor at zero) and forwards forges pull one analysis a few hours early, once
78. 2026-08-11 · Coins ship with NO shop. 1 coin per day you spoke, append-only `earned`/`spent` ledger, price of the first (unbuilt) shop item fixed NOW at 14 · deciding the price before the sink exists is the only thing that stops everyone being instantly rich when it lands — 14 coins ≈ two weeks of practice. A ledger rather than a mutable integer so the rate can be re-tuned or retro-adjusted without corrupting history. The earn rate is enforced by a DB check constraint and a partial unique index, not by the client remembering
79. 2026-08-11 · A day you spoke pays once, however many reps; a frozen day pays nothing · rewards the rep, never the login (#5 of the 11 Aug kill-list), and a freeze protected you rather than trained you
80. 2026-08-11 · Accounts finish as email + password, no social login; the anonymous upgrade attaches credentials to the SAME auth user · there is no migration, so there is nothing to drop in the migration — every rep, XP event, freeze and coin keeps pointing at the same id. Signing IN to a different account is the one path that orphans device progress, and both auth screens say so before the tap
81. 2026-08-11 · Password rule is length only (8+), no composition rules · "one symbol, one digit" measurably produces `Password1!`; a long simple passphrase is better and the copy says why
82. 2026-08-11 · Password reset links point straight at `/auth/reset`, not through the shared callback · one page working out what kind of link it is means racing the Supabase client, which consumes the URL fragment as it initialises. Choosing the destination at send time removes the race instead of timing it
83. 2026-08-11 · All transactional mail sends from `hello@speakethos.com` with a real reply-to; no `noreply@`, ever · early replies to verification mails are free user research. Every template says so in its footer so replying is an obvious move rather than a guess
84. 2026-08-11 · Coin art is ours, generated not commissioned: flat, no outlines, four warm tones, a speech-bubble emboss · four states ship (single, stack, earn-burst, empty). The empty state is inlined in the component so it can draw in `currentColor` — a fixed grey glows on the dark ground or vanishes on the cream one. Amber stays earned-only: an unearned coin is never gold
85. 2026-08-11 · Positioning is FORMAT, not features: Yoodli is named as a strong rehearsal tool that does body language; the claim is "nobody is doing daily, streak-driven, gamified reps with video" · Yoodli took a $40M Series B in Dec 2025 at ~$300M with Google and Databricks as clients and a ~300k-member Toastmasters partnership. "Nobody does body language" is false and checkable, and being caught is the fastest way to lose the one thing this product sells (asserted in tests)
86. 2026-08-11 · Two headlines: "Practice being worth listening to." on acquisition surfaces, "Earn the room." on hero/onboarding/merch · clarity converts at zero awareness; the brand line translates the name once the category is established
87. 2026-08-11 · The word "confidence" is banned from all copy, enforced by a test over `app/**` and `components/**` · vaguest possible promise, names the outcome rather than the trained thing, and every competitor uses it. **Amends #18**: the Index dimension keeps its `confidence` KEY in the schema, the prompt and every stored row — only the display label changes, to "Steadiness", which is closer to what its anchors (hedges, restarts) actually count. Label change, no migration, no re-scoring
88. 2026-08-11 · ⚠ The live delivery ring uses AMBER for its nudge, per §2 of the 11 Aug log · **flagged, not silently applied — this narrows #65.** Everywhere else amber means "you earned this"; on the ring it means "look at this", and one colour carrying both valences weakens the system. Implemented as specified because the instruction was explicit, isolated to one constant (`RING_TONE` in `app/rep/page.tsx`) so reversing it to `ring-stone-400` is a one-line change
89. 2026-08-11 · Killed, do not re-add: clan wars (needs a synchronous population; at launch it matches one user against silence), free sign-in gifts (rewards opening the app, which is the easy part), milestone chests (only as good as contents, and there are none), time-locked content (backwards at zero users — you want people to binge) · progress-gating on cleared drills stays, it's free and it's earned
90. 2026-08-11 · Deferred with reasons: the shop (its sinks mostly compete with Pro — needs its own session), VS mode (~month 3, and ASYNC if built: a ghost race against a stored rep, not matchmaking), a hired illustrator (not until revenue), the linear road on home (a 12-node path advertises how little content exists) · none of these are open questions, they're dated ones
91. 2026-08-11 · Correction happens right AFTER the rep, never mid-sentence · cutting someone off mid-sentence is bad coaching, and it is expensive streaming infrastructure for the privilege. Mid-rep interruption is not built and is not to be built (the Hostile Q&A mod's interjection is a stress mod, not a correction)
92. 2026-08-11 · The service worker never caches `/pose/` · ~25MB of WASM plus a 5.5MB model would blow the quota on a mid-range phone and take the shell down with it on eviction. The browser's HTTP cache handles repeats; a first video rep in a tunnel honestly reports the mode unavailable

93. 2026-08-11 · Home carries a DAY COUNTER — "Day 12", counting distinct days you spoke — beside the streak, plus a bare trendline of the Ethos Index by day · the streak is the right pressure and the wrong memory: it resets, so the morning after a missed day opens on the number that says you failed. This one only goes up and never resets, and it can't overstate (#47) because it counts days containing a real rep, not days since signup — miss a month and you return to Day 12, not Day 43
94. 2026-08-11 · The day trail lives INSIDE the existing score card, not in a card of its own · #9 gives the floor the screen and brand.md allows one tap; a third card would be the "not average, but best" protocol losing to feature-adding. The Log owns real charts, so this is a 34px strip
95. 2026-08-11 · A day's score is its BEST rep, and the trail's chip says "best day yet" rather than restating the arc · best-of matches how `starsByLesson` already summarises a lesson (a warm-up after a good rep shouldn't erase it). The chip was originally "+143 across 12 scored days", which sat directly under the card's "+95 since your first rep" — two numbers about the same thing, computed differently (day-bests vs rep-to-rep), disagreeing on one card. Caught by screenshotting it. "Best day yet" is strictly-greater-than, so matching your old best is holding rather than beating it

96. 2026-08-11 · Everything is free, behind ONE constant (`EVERYTHING_FREE` in `lib/entitlement.ts`) · Timothy's call — at zero users the product needs people in all of it more than it needs a conversion rate. Not a tier rethink: #31, #73 and #75 are all still implemented and tested, and `profiles.premium` is still read underneath, so turning the paywall back on is one boolean. Also fixed two limits that were constants rather than gates — the 7-day log and the 3-entry lexicon applied to premium accounts too
97. 2026-08-11 · Pause is scored on PLACEMENT, not presence · reported by Timothy: "pauses get 100 even though they were bad pauses — the code measures that pauses exist, not if they are good". It did: baseline 60, +8 per pre-sentence gap, so five slow starts scored the same as five landed points. Goldman-Eisler and Boomer split silence into JUNCTURE pauses (at a grammatical boundary — fluent production) and HESITATION pauses (inside a clause — planning difficulty); same silence, opposite meaning, and only the boundary tells you which. `lib/pause-quality.ts` classifies every held pause as landing / opening / hesitation / filled / dead, scores placement as a RATIO (60) plus usage (40), and the old baseline is gone — no held pauses at all scores 20, not 60
98. 2026-08-11 · A pause with a filler leaning on it earns nothing, and past 3.5s it costs · the whole technique is silence INSTEAD of the "um", so buying the silence back with one is not composure. Dead air stops reading as command and starts reading as lost. Both are named with a timestamp, never as a grade
99. 2026-08-11 · Amber on the pause bar follows the VERDICT, not the pause kind · a pre-sentence pause that was filled or ran to dead air is still pre-sentence, and painting it gold was the same flattery the score was guilty of. Keeps #22's intent (amber = earned) now that we can tell earned from merely present
100. 2026-08-11 · Self-corrections cost, and are detected as repeated n-grams whose landing changes · reported by Timothy: "ease the days rest" then "ease the days problems" scored as clean speech, because the old check only caught a literally doubled word. A listener hears a repair exactly like an "um", so both feed one `disfluenciesPerMin` that drives the fillers dimension AND stars. Cut points unchanged and now measuring more — due a recalibration on real recordings
101. 2026-08-11 · Each dimension is shown as its WEIGHTED points against its own denominator (132/150, not 88/100), with the bar filled to the same fraction and the sum printed · reported by Timothy: "800 is the max if logical, 8×100 is 800". The dimensions were never worth 100 each, and showing them as if they were made the /1000 look like it didn't add up. **`ethosIndex` now rounds per dimension** so the parts equal the whole exactly rather than missing by a point or two (asserted in tests) — a breakdown that doesn't add up in front of you is worse than none
102. 2026-08-11 · The list says how many dimensions are actually on it · it read "The eight" on every rep including the ones where the coach layer never ran and only four existed
103. 2026-08-11 · Results are walked one screen at a time — score, numbers, words — with no exit until the last · everything on one page meant the top was read and the rest was scrolled past on the way to Done. The log still renders all of it at once, because a stored rep is reference rather than a debrief. The ending (streak-end rule, #52) moves to the last screen, where the decision to come back is actually made
104. 2026-08-11 · Every lesson carries its technique, shown BEFORE the rep · the tips existed only inside the opt-in Frame step, which is off by default (#35) — so in practice a lesson named a target and never said how to hit it. Each tip maps to something the engine measures, so the feedback afterwards is about the same thing the tip was about
105. 2026-08-11 · The results flow ends on "Next lesson", not only "Done" · finishing a rep is the moment with the most momentum in the product and it was spending it on a link home
106. 2026-08-11 · Counters say "recordings"; "rep" stays the verb · reported by Timothy: "what does reps even mean — is 60 seconds one rep, or is everything between two pauses a rep". As a label on a number it left the unit ambiguous. The counter now names the thing it counts, and no explanatory text was added to do it
107. 2026-08-11 · The favicon is Demos, trimmed to the head · `scripts/make-favicon.mjs`. The trim is the whole trick — untrimmed, the full-body render at 16px is a beige smudge with a fleck of face in it, which is why home still has no head mark. From `public/demos.webp` so it keeps its alpha; the iOS icon is flattened because iOS composites alpha onto black

108. 2026-08-11 · Fillers and self-corrections are scored SEPARATELY — Fillers /100, Self-corrections /50 · they were briefly one blended "disfluency" score, and blending them hid which one you were actually doing. Different problems, different fixes: an "um" is a gap you filled and the cure is silence; a repair is a sentence you abandoned and the cure is finishing the one you started. The old 150 SPLITS rather than grows, so every other dimension keeps its weight and the total stays /1000 (asserted in tests). **Amends #18's eight** — the measured tier is five now, and the results label counts the rows instead of claiming a number
109. 2026-08-11 · The repair curve runs out at 3/min where fillers run out at 8 · repairs are rarer and cost more when they land — an "um" is half a second of nothing, an abandoned sentence makes a listener drop the thread and re-follow you. One repair a minute already costs a third of its dimension
110. 2026-08-11 · Stars keep reading the COMBINED rate · stars are one 1–3 signal and need one number, and to a listener both are the same kind of stumble. The Index is where the two come apart, because that's the surface with room to say which

111. 2026-08-11 · Loading states are skeletons, and a skeleton is NEVER an empty state · `/you` and `/path` initialised to `[]` and `0`, so for a few hundred milliseconds they rendered "Level 1 · 0 XP · 0 coins · 0-day streak" to someone who might have a hundred reps. That is the app breaking its own rule (client-data: honest empty states, never fake numbers) in the one place nobody thinks to check. State is `null` while in flight, `[]` when the fetch lands empty, and only `null` gets a shimmer — a permanent skeleton would be a lie in the other direction
112. 2026-08-11 · A skeleton has to match what replaces it, and locally-known content never gets one · the floor card on home comes out of `todaysDrill()` with no round trip, so it paints immediately; covering it would make the app feel slower than it is. Shapes match heights and radii so arriving content doesn't shove the page — a placeholder that causes the shift it was meant to prevent is worse than the pop
113. 2026-08-11 · The shimmer is built from `sand` and `surface`, and stops under `prefers-reduced-motion` · inherits the dark theme's redefinition of both without a second rule, and stays low-contrast on purpose: a placeholder that pulses hard competes with the content it stands in for. Reduced motion keeps the block and drops the sweep

114. 2026-08-11 · Display face is **Fraunces** (600/700), tabular figures on, tracking −0.008em · **Amends #5**, on Timothy's explicit call, which is the one way that file allows a locked decision to reopen. Space Grotesk's "1" carries a narrower advance than its glyph, so "11", "12", "31" and "118" collided on every screen in the app — and a display face whose numerals are its weakest feature cannot serve a product whose whole identity is numbers. Fraunces keeps the character, reads warm against the terracotta and cream instead of techy, and suits a name meaning credibility earned rather than claimed. Body stays Inter, data labels stay Space Mono — serif display over sans body is the classic pairing and only the display face was wrong
115. 2026-08-11 · Tabular figures are ON, reversing the workaround in #5's era · they were refused because Space Grotesk's tnum made the overlap worse. Fraunces has no such fault, so a live metric stops reflowing as its digits change — which is the entire point of tabular figures and what a screen full of counting numbers wants

116. 2026-08-11 · **The disfluency prompt in `transcribe.ts` is load-bearing and must not be "tidied".** Measured against generated speech carrying a known 3 "um" and 2 "uh": **no prompt → 0 and 0; with the prompt → 3 and 2.** Whisper deletes every non-lexical filler unless primed, and the filler count is the product's core number — without that one string the engine scores a transcript with the evidence already removed. Guarded by a test
117. 2026-08-11 · The prompt stays SHORT · a denser version was tried the same day on the theory that more priming catches more. It recovered exactly the same 3 and 2, then hallucinated "Thank you for watching." onto six seconds of near-silence where the short prompt returned nothing. A long prompt gives Whisper more to fall back on when there is no signal, and an invented sentence sails through the substance gate as a real rep. Longer is strictly worse; the length ceiling is asserted in a test
118. 2026-08-11 · ⚠ **Correction — the swallowed-filler hypothesis was WRONG.** Nine stored reps showing near-zero "um" looked like Whisper eating them, and the pattern was suggestive: the one rep whose fillers survived ("you know", a phrase Whisper keeps) had zero mid-clause held pauses, against up to ten for the others. The experiment refuted it — those reps were transcribed WITH the working prompt, so nothing was being swallowed. The stored filler counts are probably accurate: the user's fillers are "you know" and "like", not "um", and the mid-clause gaps are real silent hesitation. Kept here because the reasoning was published before it was tested, and the correction is more useful than the deletion
119. 2026-08-11 · `unvoicedHesitations` survives its own refuted premise, for a better reason · it was added to catch swallowed "um"s and there are none to catch. But the held-pause threshold is 0.8s, so a 0.3–0.8s mid-clause gap is invisible to every dimension — and that band is exactly where someone composing mid-sentence lives. Shown on the Pause row (not Fillers, which was the wrong home and carried a claim now known to be false), still unscored: the pause dimension already judges everything above the threshold, and scoring the short band needs a calibration nobody has earned

120. 2026-08-11 · The engine measures the GAPS acoustically, not just lexically — `lib/envelope.ts` · the filler count rested entirely on one string in a Whisper prompt, and a vendor's editorial habits are a fragile place for the product's core number to live. The recorder already runs an AnalyserNode for the level meter, so sampling RMS at 20Hz costs nothing and gives every inter-word gap a checkable property: energy in it = a sound you made that wasn't a word; genuinely quiet = real silence. ~7KB per rep, rides along with the audio POST
121. 2026-08-11 · A gap the mic heard a sound in can never score as composure · this is the half of the swallowed-filler problem that survived the refutation. Whisper deleting an "um" leaves an empty stretch of timeline, and the pause dimension was scoring it as silence — so the same hesitation was rewarded rather than counted. Transcribed fillers and voiced gaps cannot double-count: if Whisper wrote the "um" down, the word occupies that time and there is no gap there to classify
122. 2026-08-11 · The envelope self-calibrates per rep against its own 5th/90th percentile, and returns "unknown" rather than guessing · a quiet sofa and a loud tram must give the same verdicts with no setting to touch, and "we checked and it was silent" has to stay distinguishable from "we couldn't check" — only the first is evidence. A rep with no dynamic range, or no envelope at all, scores exactly as it did before this shipped

123. 2026-08-11 · A STYLE violation never costs a rep its Ethos Index; a GROUNDING violation still does · caught in production runtime logs — `[coach] gave up after 3 attempts: coachLine has 3 sentences; max is 2`, and the rep came back with no Index at all. Validation now splits: hard problems are false claims (a quote not in the transcript, a number not in the metrics, a judged score citing nothing) and still fail the rep, per the no-horoscope rule #19. Soft problems are house style, and are repaired — the coachLine is trimmed and the rep is kept. Trading the product's headline number for a copy rule is the wrong trade
124. 2026-08-11 · Sentence counting ignores decimal points · the splitter was `split(/[.!?]+/)`, so "1.5 fillers a minute. Kill the 'um'." counted as THREE sentences. Every coachLine is required to cite a metric and half the metrics are decimals, so the rule fired on ordinary output and was the actual cause of #123's production failure. A terminator only ends a sentence when it isn't between two digits

125. 2026-08-11 · **The shop opens** — closing the #90 deferral · reopened on Timothy's call (his girlfriend asked for it, which is the only user research this feature has and more than most features get). The objection in #90 was that every sink competed with Pro; everything is free now (#96), so it's moot. Four items: the streak freeze at 14, three Demos poses at 8/8/12. The non-negotiable survives intact because coins are earned by speaking and the shop sells only convenience and decoration — nothing on it buys a star, a streak or a point of Ethos, and that sentence is printed on every card rather than assumed
126. 2026-08-11 · Freezes are BUYABLE with coins, which amends #38's "earned, never bought" · the rule that matters is *money* never buys them, and coins are one day of speaking each. 14 coins is two weeks of practice — the same two weeks the streak path charges (#38: one freeze per full week, two maximum). Buying is a slower route to the same thing, not a shortcut past it, and a frozen day still doesn't count toward the streak either way
127. 2026-08-11 · **Amber goes back to earned-only on the rep screen** — the nudge ring is stone, the hold counter is amber · §2 shipped the live ring amber and the code flagged it in place as the one screen where the colour meant "look at this" instead of "you earned this", with a note saying which constant to flip. Flipping it also fixed the deeper problem: the screen only ever spoke up to complain. A nudge is still unmissable on three channels (ring 2px→4px, skeleton dims to stone, note appears) and loses a fourth that turns out to be the loudest — the hold run you were accumulating resets to zero
128. 2026-08-11 · You can see the skeleton of your own limbs while you speak · MediaPipe's landmarks were already being computed for Presence and then thrown away; drawing them costs one canvas. Deliberately a skeleton and not a mesh or a filter — it reads as measurement, and it makes what's being scored legible: the line between your shoulders IS the posture number, the one from your nose is the eye line. Cream when the ring is clean, stone when it isn't
129. 2026-08-11 · Live tips are TIMED to the arc of a rep, not rotated · `lib/live-tips.ts`. A carousel of advice is noise you learn to ignore, so each line fires at the fraction of the cap where it applies — opening beat, first landed point, the middle where the ums live, the last line — holds 6s, and yields entirely to a live nudge. Every line is ≤42 characters and names something the engine actually measures (test-enforced): you cannot read and speak at the same time, which is the same reason the Frame step's notes disappear when the clock starts
130. 2026-08-11 · Section headings are Fraunces sentence case (`.section-title`), not Space Mono uppercase · reported as "I don't like the subtitle font" on "Coins", "Streak freezes", "Every rep" — and the complaint was structural, not a matter of taste. Those headings were `.label-data`, the exact style used for the caption *inside* the card they introduce ("1 per day you speak"), so two levels of hierarchy printed identically and the page read as one continuous receipt. Reference is Linear: a heading earns rank from weight and colour, not decoration. `.label-data` keeps the job it's good at — labelling a number
131. 2026-08-11 · Nothing in the shop is painted terracotta · brand.md allows one terracotta tap per screen and a shop has four, but the stronger reason is the second one: painting "Buy" in the attention colour is the exact nudge a store that refuses to sell you a score shouldn't be making. The price is the argument; the button is a door. Cosmetics also show their art, because a decoration bought blind is the one way a decoration can still be a bad deal

132. 2026-08-11 · ⚠ **The coin grant had been failing silently since it shipped** — nobody ever earned a coin · `grantCoins` upserted with `onConflict: (user_id, earned_on)` against a **partial** unique index, which Postgres rejects with 42P10. The error was swallowed, `grantCoins` returned 0, `syncCoins` caught nothing, and the balance sat at zero with no symptom anywhere. Fixed in migration 0005 (full unique index on `(user_id, reason, earned_on)`) plus the matching client target — and the swallowed error now logs, because the silence is what let this live

133. 2026-08-12 · A fresh browser opens on /welcome; "fresh" = no welcomed flag AND no Supabase session in storage; the flag is set by /welcome itself and by any floor render with an existing session · the three-screen introduction (#11) existed but was orphaned — nothing routed a first visit into it, so the funnel started at a cold floor card. Reference: Duolingo's delayed-signup funnel (intro → lesson 1 → save-progress ask) is their single best published growth result (+20% DAU, Gotthilf first-hand). Screen 1 gains "I already have an account" (their splash pattern) so a returning device can sign in without spelunking /you. Full pass in docs/onboarding-research.md
134. 2026-08-12 · Rep 1's exits run through a save-progress soft wall for anonymous sessions, shown once more when the streak reaches 3, never again after · this is the gate #15 specified ("at save your progress") finally built where it was specced — it existed only at the bottom of /you. The wall stakes the just-made numbers ("Day 1 is on the board", the Index, the streak), the primary is "Save my progress", and the decline is a quiet "Not now" that continues to exactly the tapped destination — Duolingo measured a prominent "Discard my progress" decline driving users away. No hard wall: their data says soft walls need one to convert fully, but holding a rep hostage to auth breaks #15's spirit — flagged in the research doc as the first dial if anonymous day-14 retention is terrible. #103's walk and #105's ending untouched from rep 2 on
135. 2026-08-12 · Rep 1's score carries a baseline line in the delta's slot — "Day 0. Every rep after this has a number to beat." · novices tune out on failure-framed feedback (Eskreis-Winkler & Fishbach 2019, n=1,674) and Elevate's EPQ is the precedent: hard number, forward frame. The numbers themselves don't soften (#10, #47) — what changes is what the first one claims to be. The lesson was already NAMED "The baseline rep"; the frame now matches the name. The rep-1 note under the prompt also primes the mic ask in one plain sentence (permission asks at moment-of-use, primed, run 2–3× the grant rate)
136. 2026-08-12 · The last results screen offers a one-tap when-plan (morning / lunch / evening) while no reminder hour is set; the tap stores the hour, asks notification permission, and arms the reminder · implementation intentions are the largest effect in the whole research pass (d = 0.65, Gollwitzer & Sheeran meta-analysis, 94 tests) where merely asking about goals is d = 0.24 — which is why this is one tap on the way out and never a "how many minutes a day?" quiz screen (#11). The tap is also the permission primer, so the OS dialog only ever follows the user picking a time: the notification is a reminder of their own plan
137. 2026-08-12 · Anonymous sessions with ≥1 rep get ONE standing line on home — "N recordings live only in this browser · keep them" — quiet text under the boss card, never a filled card · the soft wall asks loudly twice (#134); this is the persistent honest statement of risk for everyone who declined, at the volume of "Make it harder". Loss-aversion framing allowed, guilt not (#26); brand.md keeps the floor's one terracotta tap uncontested

138. 2026-08-12 · Sound ships as ONE synthesized chime at the streak celebration, and nowhere else; hard rule: no UI sound may ever play while the mic is hot · sound follows amber's rule (#65) — it means "you earned this", so it fires at the one celebration per rep (#34) and never as furniture. The mic rule is structural, not stylistic: the envelope (#120) scores every gap during a rep, and a chime through the speakers would be counted as a sound you made. Synthesized in `lib/sfx.ts` per the crowd-noise doctrine (no asset, no CDN, works offline): rising C5 major triad, sine through a gentle lowpass, struck-not-switched envelopes; milestones land the octave on top — longer, never louder (asserted in tests). Audio is licensed by the Rec tap (browsers require a gesture), opt-out beside haptics in settings, on by default. Every constant is a v1 guess in the star-threshold bucket — a sound-design research pass is in flight and calibrates by ear on a real phone speaker, not by re-litigating the placement. Superseded in part by #505 (18 Nov): every win has its own sound, sized to it, and "one sound per take" no longer holds.

139. 2026-08-12 · The chime is calibrated to the research pass (docs/sound-research.md): D-major rising figure an octave LOWER than the first cut, mallet-ish voice (triangle +3 cents + quiet sine octave partial — earcon guidelines say instrument timbres, never raw tones), banded 180–2200 Hz for phone speakers; the daily chime stays an OPEN triad and only milestones land the resolving octave · Google's rule — the more often a sound fires, the less intrusive it must be — maps exactly onto daily-vs-milestone. **One deviation from the researched spec, on purpose**: its accent note was louder (0.70 vs 0.50 peak) and ours is capped at the daily maximum — register and length carry the accent, because louder-on-bigger is the slot-machine pattern the same brief documents (Dixon et al.), and a milestone should say more, never shout more (asserted in tests). Default stays ON with the settings opt-out — the brief's default-muted suggestion was [C]-grade and an "enable sound?" ask inside the celebration would tax the one moment #34 keeps brief

140. 2026-08-12 · **Amends #139 on Timothy's explicit call** ("incorporate the casino jingle for increased affect of arousal") — the arousal half of the casino playbook is adopted for REAL wins, and the fraud half stays banned · the accent cap reverses: the daily chime restores the researched spec's louder D5 landing (0.70), and milestones escalate past "longer" to a genuine fanfare — a fast two-octave D-major arpeggio (7 notes, ~65 ms spacing) landing on D6, the arousal profile of a jackpot. The line that does NOT move, because vision.md holds it regardless of conversion: sound fires only on a real, earned event. Dixon et al.'s measured harm is jingles on *losses* — celebration on a non-win distorting outcome memory — so there is still no sound on "not enough to score", none on a rescued streak, none unearned, one sound per rep (#34). Arousal on genuine wins is Duolingo's lesson-complete fanfare, not manipulation Superseded in part by #505 (18 Nov): a take now has a sound for each star and its biggest win; the no-sound-on-a-loss line stands (a take with no star plays nothing).

141. 2026-08-12 · **The road comes home, and the path gets its missing half** — reopens #90's "linear road on home" deferral on Timothy's explicit call ("duolingo's long winding path on the first screen makes people want to reach the end") · #90's objection was that a 12-node road advertises how little content exists; the fix is the content — the three units mechanics.md always named and nobody built (Structure, Compression, Thinking Under Fire: 20 new lessons, 29 total, every tip still mapping to something the engine measures per #104), extending #28's ladder at 16/24/32 with a test asserting every gate is affordable from daily units alone (#36's intent: the free path is never walled behind boss stars). The road itself is `PathRoad` — vertical, winding, the WHOLE path under the floor card without a tab switch, ending on the honest length ("29 lessons… about 4 weeks"), which is #90's objection inverted: with real content, the distance is the pitch. It inherits the rail's settled grammar (#44 proximity on locked units, #45's endowed node, amber earned-only, one terracotta ring on the current node = the same rep the floor button serves). The score card's one-line path link retires as duplicate surface; the horizontal `PathRail` (dead since the demotion) is deleted; /path keeps the detailed list; boss sits mid-road at its #28 gate of 12 as a checkpoint

142. 2026-08-12 · ⚠ **The save-progress upgrade never worked** — reported by Timothy as the raw server error ("Updating password of an anonymous user without an email or phone is not allowed") · #80's password-first order ("so a failed confirmation still leaves credentials to sign back in with") was rejected by GoTrue on every attempt since it shipped: a password cannot attach to an anonymous user with no email, so the funnel's most important tap errored for everyone, always. Now Supabase's documented convert sequence: attach email → confirm from the inbox → set the password on the landing page, with the link pointing straight at /auth/reset?first=1 per #82's no-race rule. The signup form for an anonymous session collects EMAIL ONLY — a password field the flow can't use is how this shipped broken and stayed broken. An expired first-link routes back to /signup, not /auth/forgot (an unconfirmed email can't receive a reset mail). Amends #80's mechanism, not its promise: same auth user, nothing migrates. Same lesson as #132: the upgrade "worked" in every test that didn't reach the real server

143. 2026-08-12 · A reminder never fires about a day whose rep is already done — when `didToday`, `nextFireTime` arms TOMORROW's hour, at every tier · reported by the overnight growth run (docs/growth/07-lifecycle.md §0.2.2): only the open-tab timer checked `didToday`, so the OS-scheduled tier pushed "12 days. One rep keeps it." hours after the rep landed. A wrong push is the fastest way to teach someone to disable the channel, and a scheduled notification can't check anything at fire time — so the honest day is chosen at arm time. Settings' "Next:" line follows the same rule, because a printed fire time the code won't honour is #42's lie in miniature
144. 2026-08-12 · Tests never pin a calendar date against the real clock · the metering tests hardcoded `accruedOn: "2026-08-11"` and went red by themselves the next day — the meter accrued a real elapsed-day grant, so the "capped" fixtures silently stopped being capped and the suite failed good code while un-testing the cap path. Fixtures now derive `accruedOn` from `dateKey(new Date())`. Same family as #132/#142: it worked in every test that didn't meet the real clock

145. 2026-08-17 · The design kit lands as **rules plus primitives, not as a palette** — `DESIGN-RULES.md` and `COPY-RULES.md` join the CLAUDE.md reading list, `components/ui/*` and `lib/motion.ts` come in, and the kit's `theme.css` is **refused** · its palette wipe proposes ember #E06E1F over terracotta #E76F51 and Bricolage/Instrument over the locked faces, which contradicts #4, #5 and #135 — and CLAUDE.md's own rule is that the brand docs override an incoming artefact every time. The kit's actual keystone argument ("the compiler becomes the auditor") is already true here: this app has never used Tailwind's default palette, so `bg-indigo-500` has nothing to compile against. Checkpoint 1 (`docs/devibe/CHECKPOINT-1.md`) measured the rest — zero gradients, zero glassmorphism, zero generic SaaS copy — so playbook phases 2–3, the de-vibe sweep, are skipped as inapplicable and the work is the premium hardening in phases 4–5. `scripts/audit-tells.sh` and the 28 baseline screenshots (`docs/devibe/before/`) come in with them, so the claim stays checkable rather than remembered

146. 2026-08-17 · The four screen states are **primitives, not per-page improvisation**: `EmptyState`, `ErrorState`/`ErrorLine`, `Overlay`, `Skeleton` in `components/ui/` · Checkpoint 1's finding was that the good version of each pattern existed in exactly one place — the paywall was the only overlay with dialog semantics, the log the only screen with a designed empty state — which is how a codebase drifts: screen one is correct and the next four are copies of the version before the fix. Two rules ride with them. **A silent `catch {}` on a read the screen depends on is banned** — it turns a dead connection into "you have done nothing", the one lie vision.md doesn't allow. And **the retry button is never terracotta**: brand.md keeps orange for the one thing a screen wants you to do, and a failed fetch is not that thing (Linear's reference — a recovery action earns its rank from position, not colour)

147. 2026-08-17 · **A read that fails says so, and offers the retry** — `lib/load.ts` puts an 8-second ceiling on every page-level read and settles it to a value the render can branch on, so a skeleton can always end · Checkpoint 1 booted the app against an unreachable backend and watched `/you` shimmer forever while home, the log, the path and the shop each rendered their ZERO state: an empty training log for someone with sixty recordings, nought stars for someone who earned twenty, "not enough coins" to someone holding thirty. Every one of those was a `.catch(() => setX([]))`, and every one of them is the lie vision.md forbids, pointed at the user's own effort. The rule that falls out: **"nothing yet" and "didn't load" are different screens**, and a number nobody could read is a dash, never a nought. The rep loop gets the version that matters most — the audio survives a failed analysis and "Score it again" re-sends it, because losing ninety seconds of speaking to one bad minute of wifi is the worst failure in the product. 8s is chosen against the same threshold Nielsen's response-time work uses for attention loss: past about ten seconds the question stops being "is it loading" and becomes "is it dead"

148. 2026-08-17 · **One focus ring, one overlay** · there was not a single `focus-visible` rule in the codebase and inputs actively removed the outline, so a keyboard user could not see where they were — now a 2px terracotta ring at 2px offset, globally, on `:focus-visible` only (never `:focus`, or the ring follows every tap). Dialog behaviour — Escape, focus trap, focus returned to the control that opened it — moves out of Paywall, the only component that ever had any of it, into `<Overlay>`; the celebration is the second screen to inherit it. The sheet focuses its own panel rather than its first button, since a sheet that opens with "Start with annual" focused is one stray Enter from a purchase nobody asked for

149. 2026-08-17 · **A chart is UI: its colours come from the theme** · the sparkline's fill and line were hexes tuned for cream, so in dark mode the Ethos Index drew a pale slab with a near-invisible trace across it (Checkpoint 1, finding 3). Two hardcoded palettes survive on purpose, with the reason written where someone will grep for it: the pose skeleton is drawn over a live camera frame, which no theme reaches, so cream reads at midnight and at noon; and the share card leaves the app, so it wears the light brand wherever it lands. The pre-paint boot script also sets `theme-color` now — the browser chrome is part of the room

150. 2026-08-17 · **A mechanic is explained where it happens, or not at all** (`COPY-RULES.md`) · budgets per screen: one em dash, one "X, not Y", one mantra, section captions of one sentence, no rhetorical fragments outside marketing. What this cost on `/you`: the coin philosophy ("a day you spoke pays once, however many reps you did…") moves to the shop, where somebody is about to spend and the fact makes the prices mean something; the freeze rules move to the two moments they're true — the home screen when a freeze is spent, and the slot caption when you have one to spend; the league loses "ranks effort, not scores" because the XP bar three sections up already says "XP = reps, never money", and saying it twice makes it a slogan instead of a rule. The test is the one in the doc: read it aloud, and if it sounds like a pitch deck or a footnote defending the roadmap, cut it. The philosophy was written for us, not for the person holding the phone. The budgets are **asserted in `lib/copy.test.ts`**, next to the existing "confidence" ban, because a budget that lives only in a document is back the first time someone writes a paywall headline in a hurry: one prose em dash per screen file (welcome carries three, one per slide), no "— which is why", and the mantra at most once. The sweep that got there touched every screen — the rep loop lost thirteen dashes, the about page five

151. 2026-08-17 · **One container level per block — `/you` gives up its boxes** · eight bordered, lifted cards down one screen is eight things of equal rank, so the eye had nowhere to land first and the page read as a receipt. Only the level card stays furniture (it answers "how far in am I"); the stat row, coins, freezes, league, lexicon and badges are now a section title and their numbers on the ground, with hierarchy from size, weight and space — Linear's discipline, the same reference that settled the section titles. The badge grid stops using 60% opacity for "not yet", which read as broken: an earned badge is full ink over an amber rule, an unearned one is stone over a hairline (Checkpoint 1, finding 8). Fewer elements, same information — the subtraction pass is the one that has to end with less on screen than it started with

152. 2026-08-17 · **One icon set, drawn in the repo** (`components/Icon.tsx`, 24px grid, 1.5px stroke, `currentColor`, no fills) — and the tab bar gets icons after all, reversing its "deliberately label-only" note · the old argument was that an icon row competes with the floor card for attention; it doesn't, because the marks are stone lines at the weight of the words under them while the floor keeps a 32px headline and the only filled colour on the screen. What icons buy is what four words in identical grey can't: a shape to aim at, on the one control people hit without reading. No icon library — four tab marks and two node marks is not a dependency, and a drawn set can be *ours* (Today is a sun over the floor line). Emoji stop being iconography: the padlock and the boss flame on the path are drawn now. The seven per-unit emoji are content, not chrome, and stay pending Timothy's ruling in `docs/devibe/needs-judgment.md` (Checkpoint 1, finding 6)

153. 2026-08-17 · **The badges become a shelf: ranked by difficulty, marked, and each one a door** — Timothy's call · three changes, one idea. Order is difficulty, easiest first, and **no tier label appears anywhere** — the position is the claim, so the list reads as a ladder and the fortnight streak closes it as the north star (#28). Every badge carries a mark from the app's own set, keyed by what it MEASURES rather than by badge, so the flame is streaks in both places it appears and the line is the Index in both. And every row links to the drill that produces its number ("Comfortable silence" opens Hold one second): a locked badge that only describes itself is a taunt, and this app's whole answer to "what do I do about it" is a rep. The two-column grid goes with it — a ranked list read left-right-left-right isn't a ranking. Order is asserted in `achievements.test.ts`, so reordering stays a decision rather than a slip

154. 2026-08-17 · **Zero em dashes, and every string cut to the shortest true version** — Timothy's call, amending #150's budget of one · one-per-screen needed a spreadsheet to enforce and still let the tic through; zero is a rule anyone can check, and the substitutes (comma, full stop, colon) are shorter anyway. The sweep ran through the content libraries this time, not just the screens: 27 drills lost their long-form tips, the pause notes and Presence moments swapped the dash separator for the app's mono middot, and the rep, settings, boss, log and marketing screens lost a paragraph's worth of prose each. **The ban is written into the coach's system prompt too**, with "say it in as few words as possible", because half the copy in a finished rep is generated and a rule the model doesn't see is a rule that reaches the user anyway. The reason under all of it: someone opened this app to speak, and every sentence they read first is a sentence they didn't spend speaking. Asserted per-file in `lib/copy.test.ts` across app, components and lib. The one dash that stays is `—` standing alone for a number nobody could read, which is what a dash is for

155. 2026-08-21 · **Home and the path finish merging: the Path tab retires, /path redirects home** · Timothy's call ("home page is do the next lesson plus a mini path — combine them"). #141 already put the whole road on home, which left the tab a second door to the same room, and #57's lesson applies in reverse: duplicate surface is drift too. The precedent is exact — Duolingo's Nov 2022 redesign folded the tree INTO home (stated goal "decreasing confusion"; future content stays visible, greyed, on the same scroll) and cut a tab doing it. What the list page had that the road lacked moves rather than dies: each unit's blurb now sits under its gate's name. The route stays as a redirect so bookmarks and the cached shell land somewhere real
156. 2026-08-21 · **Unit boundaries on the road are GATES: a drawn door, closed until the star total opens it** · Timothy's ask ("lock it behind a giant door, a gated vibe"), built inside the locked rules: a gate opens on cumulative stars (#28) and nothing else — #89's kill of time-locks stands, and unlike Duolingo's "JUMP HERE" there is no walking past a closed door, because progression is earned by measured quality (#10). The door is drawn in the icon grammar (#152: 1.5px stroke, currentColor, no fills), its open and closed states are geometry rather than animation, and the caption keeps #44's exact distance ("opens at 16 stars · 4 to go"). It becomes the road's ONE lock symbol: locked lessons stay visible and dimmed beyond it (#141's whole-road rule, Duolingo's greyed future path), but the per-node padlocks go — a mechanic is explained where it happens, once, and it happens at the gate. Closed gates are stone-500, open ones recede to stone-300; amber stays earned-only and the door never wears it. IconLocked and IconPath leave the set with their jobs
157. 2026-08-21 · **The fourth tab is Games: a menu of real reps under staged conditions** · Timothy's call ("a mini game tab with mini games like qa, speed rush, interview"). The label is Elevate's — the locked metric-presentation reference names its library tab Games. Three ship because three can run today (#36's honesty rule, asserted in `lib/games.test.ts`): Q&A stages the existing interruption effect, Speed rush the existing 45s tight timer, Interview is ten classic questions through the unchanged engine. Yoodli-style conversational follow-ups are deliberately out of scope — one real interruption beats a simulated interviewer we can't run — and the full multi-question Hostile Q&A stays boss territory (#13). A game rep is `kind: "daily"` so streaks, coins and the judged meter are untouched (#76's spirit); XP multiplies with the staged mods (#37); stars stay measured (#10); and the resolver refuses to label a rep with a game whose mods the account didn't run — the name never outruns the conditions (asserted in tests). Menu grammar is the shop's (#131): no terracotta, rows are doors, and the question is drawn on tap rather than shown, because the cold open is the training (#59's roulette reasoning)

159. 2026-08-21 · **Traits: the nine Index dimensions become levels on the profile, earned by performance and derived, never stored** · Timothy's call ("best traits as bars; level up a trait after each rep, occasionally a double for really good performance"). The rule: a rep's single best dimension levels +1 at 70% of its points, +2 at 90%; below the bar nothing levels, because a level is #10's kind of earned or it doesn't exist. Duolingo's crown levels are the cautionary reference — per-skill levels that rose on attendance rewarded repetition and stood up a rival progression, and both faults retired them — so traits are a mirror, not a currency: they gate nothing, buy nothing and feed nothing, and the three-currencies rule stays three. Levels are DERIVED from stored reps on every load (#132's lesson: a stored counter rots silently, a derivation is wrong out loud), which also back-fills every rep already in the log the moment this ships; the substance gate is re-run on the stored transcript, so the #54 rep ("I don't know" eight times, clean pace) can never level Pace. One trait per rep, ties broken by canonical order so the same rep always levels the same trait. Asserted in `lib/traits.test.ts`
160. 2026-08-21 · **Where traits show: ranked bars on /you, one quiet line in the results walk** · Elevate's Performance surface is the display reference (a score per skill, your best named); its habit of mixing play-frequency into the number is exactly what #159 refuses. All nine traits show, best first, each bar scaled to the leader — amber on the leader only, unleveled traits dimmed at the bottom (the shelf's grammar, #153: position is the claim, and the printed level keeps #47 honest). The level-up line sits under the score in the points grammar the dimension list already speaks ("Structure leveled twice · 141/150 this rep"), renders identically on the stored rep in the log because it's derived, and is never a second celebration: #34 keeps the streak moment the only one
161. 2026-08-21 · **The profile gains its one typed field: a display name, edited inline on /you** · 24 characters (the league row is the widest place it prints), empty clears it, upserted under the existing own-profile RLS so no migration ships. Everything else on the profile stays earned rather than typed: poses come from the shop, traits from reps

162. 2026-08-21 · **The copywriting pass: shorter, front-loaded, and one boast per claim** · Timothy's call ("copy write the site, the language, the long winded explanations"), run against a research pass (conversion copy: clarity beats cleverness, specificity, front-load the point because ~79% of readers skim; the Wikipedia signs-of-AI-writing list via the humanizer skill) with COPY-RULES.md still the law on top. What it caught, by class: a BANNED construction on the landing hero ("Not nerves. Not a lack of ideas. Just no reps." is the exact chained-fragment tell the rules name); the signature boast said three times ("nobody else measures" was on the landing page, the welcome carousel and the results screen — it now lives once, on welcome's Earn-the-room screen, and the landing page argues it instead: "the difference is measurable, so we measure it"); justification copy cut where a string defended the design (the repairs row's "rarer and costlier, so the scale runs out at 3/min" becomes the same scale grammar the fillers row uses; the save-progress card's attach-in-place mechanics become "An account keeps them"); and two STALE claims fixed, which is the pass earning its keep: the landing page still sold "Eight dimensions. Four measured" a week after #108 made it nine and five, and the capped-rep footer hardcoded "the four measured dimensions" beside five rendered rows, now counting `rows.length` per #102. Removed: the XP mantra on /you (the mantra now prints in exactly one place, the paywall footer), the traits caption, both Games frame lines, the road's star sentence, the gate blurbs (amends #155: they retire rather than move), the freeze prose (only the days-to-go count remains), the shop's per-card `honest` lines and footer disclaimer (amends #125's "printed on every card": no-pay-to-win stays enforced by the ledger and asserted in `shop.test.ts`, it just stops being said), home's pre-first hint, and ModeToggle's why-clause. Surfaces that are dark in BOTH themes opt out of the swap entirely: the score card's captions get a non-swapping token (`sage-mist` #b6c19c) and the stage cards set their text in cream opacities — the stone ramp is translucent ink now, and translucent ink on an always-dark card is invisible exactly half the time
168. 2026-08-25 · The day trail becomes **pebbles**: one 14px dot per recent day — spoken = sage fill, frozen = sage outline, missed = faint — replacing #93's bare index trendline inside the score card · the pebble is the redesign's motif, and the strip now answers "did I show up" at a glance, which is the question the streak-and-memory pair actually asks; the index arc still has a home because the Log owns real charts (#94).
