The first-run redirect left the graphics boot's alive mark behind, so every new phone's room ran lite, with reduced motion and a false crash report; check-room and check-onboarding found it (41ea218). The splash landed on the bare streak count and then ticked (fcb8f85, 27a8506). Deferred, with reasons in the audit: 4 (a deploy would show a second open late). 7 (needs Supabase's preflight cache answered). 9 (Timothy's security call). 10 (a migration and backfill: 50 KB of Today's critical read for 22 takes, about 200 KB at 90; proposal: scorable and word_count on reps). 15, 16 and 20 (they wait for a week of stage logs). 19 (client hooks would need server wrappers; medium risk). And 18's Supabase package split. Shots in docs/look/speed/ (camera scripts/look-speed.mjs): nothing looks different, it only arrives sooner. Ref: docs/speed/audit.md, docs/speed/pre-fixes.json, docs/speed/post-fixes.json, #433, #497, #500. #508 review: Sign out everywhere now clears what the phone kept, as Sign out does (the numbers kept between opens, and the carried cue, a sentence of the person's own words). A screen left before its numbers land now lets go of what waited for its draw (expectDraw returns the leaving): leaving Today at 1.1 s had held the worker's install to its 10.6 s fallback and kept Demos's clip, the tab neighbours, the paywall's code and You's warm reads waiting for the visit; the worker now registers at 1.3 s. A recorder whose encoder refuses 32 kbps when it is made records as before #508 (the format alone, or nothing). The kept codecs are held to their reads' columns, so a new column moves KEPT_VERSION. Re-measured: a 60 s take is 174 KB (2.0 s up at 750 kbps), Today cold 4.6 s (LCP 5.1 s), You cold 4.2 s, the worker's install 675 KB in 47 files. Whisper parity (docs/speed/whisper-parity.md): five 75 s public-domain conversational clips through a local Whisper with Ethos's prompt and filler counter; 32 kbps Opus kept every clip's filler count within one of 128 kbps, inside the noise that a 20 ms shift or 1 dB of gain makes at 128 kbps; 48 kbps was not measurably safer; 32 kbps AAC (ffmpeg's encoder) sat at the edge of the noise. 32 kbps ships; the field check is the cohort's fillers a minute before and after the deploy. At the merge Say it better's spoken test (#507) moved onto speechRecorder too, so its line goes up at 32 kbps; lib/speech-recorder.test.ts found it. Left open: v21 stopped pre-caching /lessons/large, which broke #448's offline lesson art (taken up by the final pass); a press on the painted door before hydration does nothing. Final pass, 18 Nov: offline, the worker answers a lesson picture it never fetched with the best cut of that lesson it holds (720, 560, then the shell's 360), so #448's tunnel promise holds again at no install cost (public/sw.js lessonStandIn; SHELL unchanged at 47 files). The room's door keeps a finger put down before its code has run (the head script's window.__roomPress) and fills from when it landed; a press lifted before the code runs is still lost, since iOS needs the gesture to open the room with sound. The server-drawn door hides Demos's bubble, holding its space, until the code runs (it showed an empty white bubble). check-motion reads the scrim's fade from before the tap (the lazy paywall lands about 300 ms after a first tap); measure-speed's Today door is Hostile Q&A. All 33 gates passed on the final build, then 18 again under an independent review whose verdict was ship; vitest 6,169. The review also measured LazyPaywall deciding before Today's draw and reverted its fix for no measured gain.

509. 2026-11-18 · **Say it better's proof is a token under the score, outside the words** · Timothy, 18 Nov, on the three proof variants: "give it ur best shot". Three judges scored B (a token under the score) 21, C (Demos says it) 18.5, A (#507's sage line inside the ceiling) 17.5. B is built for real, and A, C and the camera-only switch are gone. On the take that first said a saved swap's better phrase (once a swap, heard_at, decided at the glance's first frame from the lists held when the results landed), a pale sage card stands under the delta. It holds a check, the better phrase at 17/700 over the said one struck through at 13 in stone-600, and its own 44pt chip that plays where it was said (`proofToken`, `fitGlanceProof`, `.proof-token`). Grafted from the others: C's spoken name ("the point is, instead of the thing is"); the chip bound to the better phrase's line; A's long-pair rule (struck always to 18 and 23 characters, from 375 wide to 24 and 32, never past, tested on the longest pair); the token named in code as the ceiling's one exception (`OUTSIDE_CEILING`, which look-glance.mjs reads); and Demos's cheer and the round sound only when the proof is the top moment (`proofLeads`; boss, level, lesson and best outrank it in `topMoment`, so one top cue). Fixed from the judges: the door opens the swap's own sheet over the results (no exit, no streak moment, no Remove, Done back to the token); door and chip are siblings with no chevron, and the chip never opens the sheet; one 20 ms pulse when it lands; the dark fill is sage-600 mixed into the ground; a sage-600 hairline under Increase Contrast; no press scale under reduced motion; the worst case (longest pair, a level-up, the first take) fits 390x844 and 320x568 with no scroll, Demos at 93 at 390. What it breaks: #507's "waiting rather than cost the cue" (the token never waits and costs no word, because the ceiling counts without it); on a proof take Demos's moment is plain text, so the glance keeps one playing chip; under 700pt Demos stands beside his line at 112, and steps off where the token meets a question or an under line; under 600pt the token is one line, the struck phrase hidden and the longest pair cut with an ellipsis; a late read of the lists shows nothing for that take; a tester's glance at 320x568 scrolls 50 (12 before). Refs: docs/look/proof/after/, docs/look/proof/sheet.jpg (live 5c1ef68 beside the token), docs/look/proof/variants/, lib/glance.ts, scripts/look-proof.mjs. #509 review: four fixes, each found with a probe and held by a test. A Retake, Practice 2 of 3 or the next lesson no longer proves the same swap twice (the screen's own read marks the swap heard, lib/say.ts heardIn). The swaps are read for every take, so the coach's pair just given and said on the Retake gets its token. The proof's round sound waits for the take's rewards, so a level-up or best landing late keeps its fanfare (lib/sound-recipes.ts withProof). The door opens the swap's sheet at once on what the take proved (provedView), not after Say it better's whole read. Left open: a second swap said in the same take, or one said where no glance showed it, is marked heard by the next Say it better read and never gets its token (heard_at does two jobs; splitting them is a product call); at 320x568 the token is one line with the struck phrase hidden. Final pass, 19 Nov: Say it better counts only takes with an Ethos number, as the glance shows the token only beside one (usesOf skips a null ethos_index). That covers a take with no star and a scored take whose judged read did not run (a free account past its one read of the day, or a coach that failed or ran out of time); neither is a use or marks the swap heard, so the token always waits for a take with a number. Open for Timothy: this under-counts a free account's later takes that day; counting every take with stars means changing the count and the glance's gate together.

510. 2026-11-18 · **A recording with no star pays no XP** · Timothy, 18 Nov, on "a recording with no stars still earns 10 XP": "drop it". `xpForTake` (lib/xp.ts) pays 0 for a take with 0 stars and `xpPaid` of its multiplier otherwise. `saveRep` (lib/db.ts) is the one place every path pays (the daily, a lesson, the boss, a game round, Hostile, an upload): it reads the measured stars, writes no `xp_events` row for a 0 and returns what it paid, and /api/analyze answers that as `xp` (0 when nothing was stored). Every surface reads that figure or the stored rows. The round card's XP line says the server's amount and drops away on a no-star round (before: "+20 XP, ×2 for the cut-in"). A stored take with no star stops naming its multiplier. You's XP today, its tick and its sound count only rows written. The reach line names star 1's goal: "Speak 20+ words today for 10 XP.", "Speak 20+ words again to reach level 21.". Stored xp_events are never rewritten (#422). The streak is unchanged: a no-star take still lights the day (Open). Tests: lib/xp.test.ts, lib/db.test.ts, lib/analyze-route.test.ts (six paths), lib/round-copy.test.ts. Shots: docs/look/xp-read/ (the game0 round card before and after). #510 review: You's reach line says "again" only once a take has paid XP today (lib/xp.ts reachLineFor), so a day of one no-star take no longer reads "Speak 20+ words again". Left open: the streak still counts a no-star take; stars follow Whisper's word list while the scored-or-not read follows the transcript, so where they differ a take can be 0 stars and still scored for traits.

511. 2026-11-18 · **Each recording keeps what its words came to: the lists read three numbers, not every transcript** · Timothy approved the speed audit's fix 10 (18 Nov). Migration 0037 adds `reps.scorable`, `word_count` and `distinct_words`, nullable and only if missing. It fills, once, the rows where any is empty from their transcripts by lib/metrics.ts `substance`'s own rule: JavaScript's lowercase for the three characters that land in a word (A to Z, the Kelvin sign, I with a dot above), words spelt letter by letter so no collation widens them, the ratio in double precision with Math.round's line at 299.5. The audit named two columns. Variety reads distinct words over words, so without a third no list could leave the transcript behind. lib/migrations.test.ts proves the fill equals `substance` on PGlite over the edges and 300 seeded texts, run twice, never touching a server-written row; check-testers-sql covers it (154 ok). /api/analyze writes all three through `saveRep` from the substance the take was scored on, on every storing path. The readers (`measured`, `readTraitsFromRow`, the trait XP gate, Today's foot, the filler trend, the reminder's practice) take the columns, or a row's own transcript where one is still empty (lib/rep-substance.ts). The lists' one read (lib/client-data.ts `LIST_COLUMNS`: Today, You, the Log, Practice, the recorder) drops the transcript and every column no list draws. `RepRow` is the list row and `StoredRep` the whole one, so tsc proves no list reader reaches a dropped column. Until 0037 is applied a read meets 42703 and goes back to the old select, once a page load; both are tested, and check-today passes either way. Today's read on the 22-recording fixture account, medians of three on the lab profile: 50,254 B to 26,229 B (-48%), 1,252 to 765 ms cold, 338 to 220 ms warm; Today drawn 4,636 to 4,072 ms cold, 1,503 to 1,339 ms warm. No score moves (#422). #511 review: the three columns are counted from the stored transcript (substance(transcript) in saveRep), not the engine's word list, so a row reads the same through its columns as through its text whichever path reads it (lib/db.test.ts; lib/list-read-parity.test.ts runs every list reader over whole rows and LIST_COLUMNS, filled and empty). Order at ship: deploy, then apply 0037, then check that no row has an empty column.

512. 2026-11-18 · **Scale: what breaks at 1k, 10k and 100k daily users, and the safe fixes in** · Timothy sent a reel about vibe-coded apps that fall over once they get users, read as: make sure Ethos holds up as it grows. Three lenses (database; server and models; storage and operations) each had their findings checked by a skeptic. The write-up is docs/scale/audit.md: one screen by size, then every finding with its evidence, breaking point and fix. The limits set by hand break first: the guest ceiling at 20 to 25 new guests an hour, Supabase's email limit at about 30 sign-ups an hour, Anthropic's Start cap at about 290 daily users, and Vercel Hobby at the first payment. whisper-1 retires on 26 Feb 2027 at any size. Models are 95 to 99% of the bill, at $0.074 per daily user per day (±50%). Built on wt-scale, each with a test that fails on the old code. Migration 0038 (0037 is #511's): the advisor's fifteen policies call auth.uid() once per statement. They were proven to allow and refuse the same on PGlite, both by Postgres's own print of each and by every browser read and write before and after. It adds lexicon (user_id, created_at desc), the three rep_id indexes, rate_limits (at), and private.sweep_kept_rows hourly at minute 31 for limiter rows past 48 h and pg_cron history past 7 days. docs/scale/explain.txt holds the plans before and after: Say it better's pair read went from 162-170 ms to 0.6-4 ms on 21k pairs, and Delete my recordings for a 365-recording account from 2,227 ms to 37 ms. Every browser read of the account's own rows names the account (lib/own-rows.ts), which alone takes the pair read to 3-6 ms before 0038. Whisper has a deadline (50 s from the request on analyze, 60 on Hostile, 20 on Say it better) and one jittered retry on a 429, a 5xx or a dropped connection. A 400 that names the model is ours: a 502 the outbox holds, never a 422 that throws the take away (lib/whisper-fault.ts). Hostile's question (25 s, then a written one) and verdict (by 100 s), the boss fact-check (the coach's 45 s) and the word lookup (20 s) all end inside their route's own time; say and words run in 30 s, not 300. The reminder sender sends 16 at a time (lib/lanes.ts): about 15k subscriptions in the busiest hour, where about 1,000 broke it. The take's stage line carries the coach's billed tokens and a failed coach call's status and type, and each refusal at the guest ceiling writes limit.guestall. Not built: anything that changes cost, a plan, retention or a product behaviour (Timothy's ten calls, at the end of the audit). Also not built: the meter race (M6, next: a claim before the coach), a take_log table, cache headers, and the 503 on an Auth outage. Ref: docs/scale/audit.md, docs/scale/explain.txt, scripts/explain-scale.mjs, #508, #511. #512 review: Adversarial review of wt-scale: ships at faf28c6. 0038 was checked on PGlite independently of its own test. Exactly the fifteen policies changed, each the old text with auth.uid() wrapped (same command, roles, using and with check), and nothing else moved but five indexes and private.sweep_kept_rows. Running it twice changes nothing, and #511's 0037 lands the same before or after it. The sweep can never take a row a limit still counts (every window is 24 h at most; it takes rows past 48 h) and touches nothing a user owns. One fix (b2fd8c6): analyze gave Whisper "now + 5 s" after a slow upload, which could pass the 60 s kill; it now ends by 55 s from the request and answers 502 with the slot back, proved by a test that failed before. Refuted: a retry that costs more than the resend it replaces, a model 400 losing a take (the outbox holds every 502), and any call outliving its route. Recomputed: the model bill ($0.074 per daily user a day; coach about $0.033 warm and $0.050 cold on Opus 5), the lexicon's 1 s at 150k recordings, 2.6 GB of audio a day at 10k, and the 16-lane sender's roughly 15k. Order at ship: deploy, then 0038. check-today's 320x568 failure predates the branch (base fails it identically). Ref: docs/scale/audit.md, lib/analyze-route.test.ts.

514. 2026-11-19 · **A free account gets 3 scored recordings a day** · Timothy, 19 Nov, on free accounts: "a free account gets a score 3 times a day max", then "Nah give them a few recordings yo". lib/metering.ts DAILY_GRANT 3 and MAX_BALANCE 3: a first-ever account starts at 3, any new day on the person's own calendar tops the balance back up to 3 (an old-rule balance of 1 included), and nothing banks past it; the date rules and the offset clamp are unchanged, Premium stays unmetered, and streaks, stars, XP and the measured tier are untouched. A scored recording is one with its Ethos score and Demos's read; an empty take or a failed coach call costs none. The fourth take of a day is measured in full and stored with no Ethos score, and its glance now says when the score comes back: "Ethos score back tomorrow" in grey before day 3 (it said "Measured, not judged."), and the door "Ethos score back tomorrow, or go Premium" from day 3 (it said "Premium reads every recording"; 33 words at the ceiling of 33 with the cue kept, one line at 320). Every number's capped section reads "Your Ethos score and Demos's read are back tomorrow. This one still counted toward your streak." Every line that states the allowance is built from DAILY_GRANT in lib/allowance-copy.ts: the paywall's and the Explore sheet's first benefit "Every recording scored (free: 3 a day)", Since day one's "scores 3 recordings a day", the door's opener "3 scored recordings a day free"; lib/allowance-copy.test.ts reads 14 surfaces for the old wording. Breaks #75's roll-over to 3: 3 a day already gives every day what the bank gave a Saturday. Left: on the day this ships, an account that already opened its day under the old rule keeps that day's balance until its own midnight; profiles.judged_rollover's default and 0013's insert still write 1 (read only for a first-ever account, which accrue sets to 3). Camera: scripts/look-glance.mjs capped and capped-early. Shots: docs/look/free3/. Ref: #75, #427, #509, decisions 11 Aug §3. #514 review: the day's 3 hold when takes arrive at once. The meter was read beside Whisper and written after the coach, so two takes sent together with 1 left were both judged, and at 3 a day a script could score its whole 30-take limit (the scale audit's M6). A free account's read is now claimed before Demos reads by a compare-and-swap on the balance and day it was read at (lib/metering.ts claimRead, lib/db.ts swapMeterState); the loser reads again and meters what is left, a store that does not answer degrades open as before, and giveBackRead returns the read when the coach fails or a second send of a take loses the save (never to a day already topped up, never past 3). writeMeterState, the unconditional upsert, is gone. Tests: 2 at once with 1 left get 1, 5 at once on a fresh day get 3, one take sent twice at once costs 1, and 200 forged-clock runs never score a meter day past 3. Cost: the measured stage waits one database round trip. Left for Timothy: Hostile and upload takes spend from the same 3. Left: profiles.judged_rollover still defaults to 1 (read only for a first-ever account, which gets 3).

words a minute's "nearer 145" is never colour alone); a held row, an unknown and a pace as far from 145 at both ends have no verdict, no colour and no mark; one negation on the save wall: "Nothing moves, so nothing can go missing." is cut and "in this browser and nowhere else" is "only in this browser", and a take with no Ethos (too short to score, or one the coach failed to read) drops "its score" from its line (`saveWallLine`); the stage's inks are colour-mixed from `--color-cream`; A's `:has()` 100dvh trick is gone and a save stage never shrinks under its contents; a capped take's label is said once to a screen reader. **Grafts refused:** a stage fallback when the card cannot be drawn (there is nothing true to stand on it, so the moment waits instead); C's "— → 662" row for a day 1 with no Ethos (a row that compares nothing is clutter on the paywall's one placement); a line or sparkline on the save wall (one recording is a point, and the shared rows fill it); C's two-column board and underline on day 1, and B's line end labels. **The gate:** scripts/check-gaps.mjs reaches both screens by a take and the header's back (since, since-wpm, since-worse, since-noindex, save-rep1, save-streak, save-noindex, save-unscored, and since the review since-d3, since-d3-pause and since-flat, at 390x844 in both themes, 430x932 and, since the review, 320x692 under an iPhone's insets) and measures each twice, its ground as every screen's and the inside of its stage (`stageBands`, a drawn line by its strokes' extent): on the build of 27 Nov all 24 ground checks fail. Its first scenes all drew the fixture's wide story, so a band the data makes (day 3's small change) passed it until the review added the day-3 and flat scenes. Camera: no band over 64 and no scroll at any of the five sizes in either theme (ground bands 32 at 390x844 and 430x932, 16 at 375x667 and 320x568, 42 at 320x692), the entrance seeked at 0, 80, 160, 240 and 400ms (docs/look/since-day-one/motion.jpg: the stage fades in over 200ms, the hero rises, the line wipes left to right, the dot lands at 820; reduced motion is fades only). Strings added: "Day 1", "Day N"; changed: the save wall's paragraph as above. It closes #521's "Left open: Since day one has two bands of bare ground". Tests: lib/since-day-one.test.ts, lib/pro-moment.test.ts. Sheets: docs/look/since-day-one/final.jpg, motion.jpg. Left open: the hero number's "/1000" is balanced by a 40px pad; a free account past 90 recordings sees "Day 1" as the oldest of the newest 90 (fetchReps' limit); the foot counts every recording on the list while the line draws only measured ones. **Review (28 Nov), six findings, each reproduced first:** (1) a change under its floor drew a thin line in a tall plot, 71 and 115pt of dark over it for day 3's Ethos 598 to 615 at 390x844 and 430x932, 74 and 114 for pauses 1 to 2, 80 and 133 for a flat line: the line now stands in its `cover` of the room, the claim's figures take what it leaves and on a tall phone the ledger's rows a share (whole for a wide pair, half for a short one), the hero's rest 0.75, 1 and 1.5 parts over the line, between and under the head; after, 38 and 50, 32 and 44, 48 and 60; the hero is a size container with a basis of its least, since one sized from its content reads 0 for 100cqh in Chrome; (2) the hidden chart kept its room, 81pt over the eyebrow at 320x692 and 68 at 320x568: it gives the room back (44 and 35); (3) worse and no verdict differed by colour alone: worse wears a broken bar; (4) a first take the coach failed was told its score is kept: `saveWallLine` asks for an Ethos number; (5) two short takes on days 1 and 2 and two measured on day 3 opened the moment with "Day 1" under both numbers: `sinceStands`; (6) at 320x568 the ledger's bar sat on the next row's rule: under 600pt it sits 2px down, and under 700pt the claim's 4px down, clear of "Day N". The camera (scripts/look-since-day-one.mjs, fifteen scenes) borrows `stageBands` and holds the inside of the stage at 390x844, 430x932 and 320x692, every sage or rust figure's mark and its clearance, and a save wall's words; on the build of 27 Nov it fails 140 checks, on this one none. Sheets: docs/look/since-day-one/review.jpg. Ref: docs/refs/wellspoken/03-course.png (art over a centred head), docs/refs/duolingo (one earned number shines), #11, #134, #232, #368, #454, #495, #496, #521.
